import json


def count_words(text):
    return len(text.split())


scenario_sets = []


# Set 1
stem_1 = """TimberCraft is a primary sector forestry business founded 8 years ago by Marcus Vance. Based in Country X, TimberCraft employs 35 full-time loggers to fell timber and supply raw logs to construction firms. Over the past three years, Country X has experienced rapid industrialization, with the secondary and tertiary sectors growing while the primary sector contracts. To capture higher added value, Marcus is planning a $250,000 expansion to build a processing mill that turns raw logs into pre-cut wooden beams for house builders. To fund this, Marcus is considering converting TimberCraft from a sole trader into a Private Limited Company (LTD) by selling shares to family members. However, local environmental pressure groups are protesting against increased tree felling, creating conflicts between Marcus's profit objectives and the local community's social objectives."""

A disadvantage is the legal incorporation fees and administrative formalities [k], which increase initial expenses [app] and delay setting up the new processing operations [an]. Overall, the advantages are greater because investing $250,000 in industrial machinery carries significant financial risk [eval], making limited liability essential to safeguard personal wealth [eval].",

stems = {
    "P1_U1_SET_01": """TimberCraft is a primary sector forestry business founded 8 years ago by Marcus Vance. Based in Country X, TimberCraft employs 35 full-time loggers to fell timber and supply raw logs to construction firms. Over the past three years, Country X has experienced rapid industrialization, with the secondary and tertiary sectors growing while the primary sector contracts. To capture higher added value, Marcus is planning a $250,000 expansion to build a processing mill that turns raw logs into pre-cut wooden beams for house builders. To fund this, Marcus is considering converting TimberCraft from a sole trader into a Private Limited Company (LTD) by selling shares to family members. However, local environmental pressure groups are protesting against increased tree felling, creating conflicts between Marcus's profit objectives and the local community's social objectives.""",


    "P1_U1_SET_02": """Artisan Bakes is a bakery business operating for 5 years as a partnership owned by Elena and Sophia. Located in a busy town center, Artisan Bakes employs 8 skilled bakers to produce hand-crafted sourdough bread and fresh pastries. The business generates $180,000 in annual sales revenue, but rising organic flour costs have reduced profit margins. Elena and Sophia want to expand by opening a second retail shop costing $90,000. To secure a bank loan for this expansion, they must present a detailed business plan to commercial lenders. However, Sophia prefers internal organic growth by expanding their existing bakery kitchen, whereas Elena advocates taking over a nearby struggling cafe through horizontal integration. The partners face a crucial strategic decision regarding their business growth strategy while managing limited financial capital.""",


    "P1_U1_SET_03": """Solis Energy Solutions is a private limited company (LTD) that has provided solar panel installation and maintenance services for 6 years. The business employs 42 certified technicians and has total capital employed of $1.2 million. Solis operates primarily in the private sector, serving residential homeowner clients. However, the local government has recently invited tenders for a $400,000 public sector contract to install solar systems across fifteen state schools. Managing Director Priya Patel is eager to bid for this contract to secure business growth and steady revenue. However, shareholders are concerned that bidding a low price to beat commercial competitors will lower profit margins and reduce annual dividend payouts, highlighting a conflict between shareholder return objectives and public sector growth ambitions.""",


    "P1_U1_SET_04": """EcoWash Solutions is a social enterprise founded 3 years ago by Omar Hassan to provide eco-friendly, waterless car detailing services. The business employs 12 previously unemployed young people, providing them with structured vocational skills and steady wages. EcoWash received an initial $80,000 start-up grant from the national government and currently generates $210,000 in annual output value. Rather than maximizing profits for private owners, EcoWash reinvests 80% of its surplus financial returns into expanded job training facilities and community environmental projects. Demand for eco-friendly car care is growing rapidly, but Omar faces an operational dilemma: whether to allocate limited cash reserves toward purchasing advanced automated cleaning equipment or hiring five additional trainees to satisfy its core social objectives.""",


    "P1_U1_SET_05": """SwiftBite Express is a fast-food delivery franchise operated by franchisee David Chen for 2 years under a master agreement with SpeedEats Corp. SwiftBite employs 15 motorcycle delivery drivers and generates $350,000 in annual revenue delivering hot meals to suburban households. To acquire the franchise, David paid an initial fee of $60,000 and must pay SpeedEats an ongoing royalty fee of 5% on gross sales. While the recognized brand name helps attract local customers, David is struggling with high fuel costs and strict operational rules imposed by SpeedEats, which restrict him from modifying meal menus or delivery pricing. David is evaluating whether the protection and marketing support of a franchise system outweigh the high fees and reduced entrepreneurial independence.""",


    "P1_U1_SET_06": """Apex Precision Engineering is a joint venture established 4 years ago between TechAuto Inc and LocalMetals Corp. The enterprise employs 65 factory workers to manufacture specialized steel components for automobile assembly plants, generating $1.5 million in annual output value. Both parent companies contributed $250,000 in financial capital, sharing ownership and management control equally on a 50/50 basis. Apex is experiencing rapid growth, but faces severe factory capacity constraints. Managing directors from both parent firms are debating how to expand production. TechAuto wants to invest $400,000 in automated robotic machinery to increase factory output, while LocalMetals prefers hiring 20 additional assembly line workers. Disagreements over capital expenditure and strategic direction are causing management conflicts and operational delays.""",


    "P1_U1_SET_07": """FreshHarvest Farms is a sole trader business owned and operated by Hannah Brooks for 10 years in a rural agricultural region. Employing 6 agricultural workers, FreshHarvest grows organic vegetables and sells them directly to local consumers at farm markets, generating $120,000 in annual sales revenue. A major national supermarket chain has recently offered Hannah a lucrative supply contract worth $150,000 per year, which would require buying new harvesting machinery costing $40,000. However, accepting the contract would force Hannah to abandon her direct customer connections and risk over-dependence on a single buyer. Hannah must weigh the opportunity cost of remaining a small, independent farm business against the financial rewards and substantial operational risks of large-scale commercial expansion.""",


    "P1_U1_SET_08": """UrbanFit Gyms is a private limited company (LTD) operating 3 commercial fitness centers for 7 years. The company employs 25 personal trainers and fitness instructors, generating $800,000 in total annual revenue. To achieve market dominance, the board of directors is considering a $600,000 takeover of a rival local gym chain operating 2 centers. This horizontal integration would significantly increase UrbanFit's market share and customer membership base. However, personal trainers at the rival gym express severe anxiety over potential job redundancies and changes in working conditions. UrbanFit's management must balance its corporate objectives of fast business growth and higher market share against serious internal stakeholder conflicts and the financial risk of taking on additional debt.""",


    "P1_U1_SET_09": """PureDrop Beverages is a partnership founded 4 years ago by Liam and Noah to produce bottled natural mineral water. The business employs 18 factory workers and generates $450,000 in annual output value. PureDrop operates in a highly competitive drinks market dominated by large multinational beverage corporations. To scale up operations and lower unit production costs, Liam and Noah have applied for a $100,000 commercial bank loan to install an automated high-speed bottling line. However, the bank requires evidence of strong financial management, as PureDrop experienced severe cash flow shortages last year due to rapid unpaid credit sales to supermarkets. The partners must demonstrate key entrepreneurial skills to convince lenders that their expansion plans will succeed.""",


    "P1_U1_SET_10": """Nova Tech Solutions is a private limited company (LTD) that has developed specialized inventory management software for retail businesses for 5 years. Nova Tech employs 20 software engineers and generates $950,000 in annual sales revenue in the tertiary sector. To fund an ambitious $500,000 international expansion into overseas retail markets, the board of directors is considering converting Nova Tech into a Public Limited Company (PLC) by floating shares on the national stock exchange. While issuing shares to the public provides substantial equity capital without interest charges, existing shareholders fear losing management control and exposing the business to the threat of a hostile takeover by larger technology competitors."""
}


for set_id, stem in stems.items():
    wc = count_words(stem)
    print(f"{set_id}: {wc} words")

import json


def count_words(text):
    return len(text.split())

To meet surging tourist demand, Maya is planning a $75,000 expansion to purchase a commercial electric kiln and extend her workshop space. ```python
stems_11_to_20["P1_U1_SET_16"] = """SolarFleet Mobility is a joint venture established 3 years ago between commercial vehicle manufacturer EcoDrive Motors and urban transit operator CityTransport Group. Operating in the secondary manufacturing sector, SolarFleet employs 50 assembly line technicians to produce specialized electric commercial delivery vans, generating $1.1 million in annual output value.


Both parent companies contributed $300,000 in financial capital, sharing management decisions, risks, and operational control equally on a 50/50 basis. SolarFleet is experiencing rapid growth, but requires $500,000 in new financial capital to expand its assembly factory. Disagreements have emerged between the partners: EcoDrive insists on raising capital by converting SolarFleet into a private limited company (LTD), while CityTransport prefers taking out a long-term bank loan, creating strategic gridlock and management friction."""


stems_11_to_20["P1_U1_SET_17"] = """GreenCycle Tech is a social enterprise founded 4 years ago by Lucas Rossi to recycle electronic waste and refurbish used computer hardware. Operating in the tertiary service sector, GreenCycle employs 15 technicians who were previously long-term unemployed individuals, providing them with technical IT skills, accredited vocational training, and fair living wages. GreenCycle generates $220,000 in annual sales revenue.


In alignment with its social enterprise model, GreenCycle reinvests 75% of its financial surplus into local community digital literacy programs. Lucas faces an operational challenge: he must decide whether to allocate $60,000 of accumulated capital reserves toward purchasing an automated diagnostic circuit scanner to boost output or spending the funds to hire and train 6 additional unemployed youth, balancing business survival against core social objectives."""


stems_11_to_20["P1_U1_SET_20"] = """Bella Moda Boutique is a private limited company (LTD) founded 6 years ago by Isabella Santos. Operating in the tertiary retail service sector, Bella Moda employs 12 retail sales assistants across two high-street fashion stores, generating $510,000 in annual sales revenue.


Over the past two years, Bella Moda's revenue has dropped by 15% due to aggressive price competition from online fashion e-commerce retailers, shifting the company's core objective from business growth to business survival. Isabella must decide whether to secure a $120,000 bank loan to launch a digital e-commerce platform and downsize physical stores, or close one high-street shop to reduce fixed overhead costs. Isabella must navigate this strategic dilemma while managing severe cash flow pressures and avoiding total business failure."""


for set_id, stem in stems_11_to_20.items():
    wc = count_words(stem)
    print(f"{set_id}: {wc} words")

[
{
"scenario_id": "P1_U1_SET_11",
"unit": "Unit 1: Understanding Business Activity",
"scenario_stem": "CraftPottery Co. is a sole trader business owned and operated by Maya Lin for 6 years in an artisan craft village. CraftPottery employs 10 skilled pottery artisans to handcraft ceramic tableware, vases, and decorative cookware, generating $160,000 in annual sales revenue. import json


def word_count(text):
    return len(text.split())


# Draft stems and check word counts
stems = {}


stems["P1_U2_SET_01"] = (
    "A commercial gym network operates 4 health centers as a Private Limited Company, "
    "employing 12 location managers and 48 hourly-paid fitness trainers. Founded 6 years ago, "
    "the business generates $1.2m in annual sales revenue, with labor costs accounting for $450,000 "
    "of operating expenses. However, annual trainer turnover has reached 30% due to heavy reliance on "
    "unpredictable hourly wages without guaranteed income security. Fitness trainers report feeling "
    "demotivated during off-peak seasons when fewer gym classes are scheduled, leading to poor customer "
    "engagement and frequent member complaints regarding class cancellations. The HR director is "
    "evaluating whether to replace hourly pay with a fixed monthly salary or introduce an 'Employee of the Month' "
    "bonus scheme offering a $50 monetary award. Additionally, expansion plans to open 2 new gym centers "
    "require appointing new head trainers while maintaining service quality."
)


stems["P1_U2_SET_02"] = (
    "A custom furniture manufacturing enterprise operates as a Partnership with 2 workshop facilities, "
    "employing 35 skilled carpenters and 10 warehouse staff. Operating for 8 years, the firm generates $2.1m "
    "in annual revenue and pays carpenters using a piece-rate system ($45 per completed wooden table). "
    "While daily production volume is high, customer product returns due to poor finishing and flawed wood assembly "
    "have increased by 25% over the past year. Carpenters rush through assembly work to maximize individual "
    "piece-rate earnings, ignoring safety guidelines and quality inspection checks. According to Herzberg's two-factor "
    "theory, hygiene factors like workplace safety and working conditions are failing, while motivators like job recognition "
    "are absent. The partners must decide whether to replace piece-rate wages with profit-sharing schemes or implement "
    "job enrichment by giving carpenters full responsibility for entire furniture design and assembly projects."
)

    "including front-desk receptionists, housekeepers, and kitchen staff. Operating for 5 years, the company generates $3.2m "

    "general manager holding a wide span of control over 20 staff members across multiple operational departments.\n\n"
    "Recent customer satisfaction ratings have fallen significantly because general managers are overwhelmed with routine administrative "
    "tasks, leaving them insufficient time to supervise guest room services or respond to customer complaints. Managing "
    "directors are analyzing whether to create a taller organizational hierarchy by appointing departmental supervisors "
    "or increase delegation by empowering front-desk receptionists and housekeeping staff to resolve guest complaints independently."
)


stems_refined["P1_U2_SET_05"] = (
    "A food processing factory operates as a Private Limited Company, employing 80 production line workers "
    "and 8 quality control managers. Founded 12 years ago, the enterprise generates $4.8m in annual sales revenue "
    "supplying packaged canned foods to supermarket chains. Following the unexpected retirement of 3 senior quality "
    "control managers, the factory faces critical leadership vacancies that threaten operational continuity.\n\n"
    "Senior executive managers are debating recruitment strategies to fill these positions quickly to maintain food safety "
    "standards. External recruitment through specialized employment agencies would attract experienced food technicians "
    "with fresh industry ideas, but involves high recruitment agency fees ($15,000 per hire) and lengthy advertising delays. "
    "Alternatively, internal recruitment would promote existing production line workers, boosting workforce morale and "
    "reducing recruitment costs, but requires extensive retraining in technical quality assurance procedures."
)

    "A software development studio operates as a Private Limited Company, employing 40 software engineers "
    "and 5 technical project managers. Operating for 4 years, the business generates $2.8m in annual sales revenue "
    "developing mobile application software, paying engineers fixed annual salaries of $65,000. Rapid expansion and "
    "new client contracts require hiring 10 additional senior software developers within two months.\n\n"
    "The HR department must design an effective candidate selection process to evaluate applicant competence. Management "
    "is debating whether to rely primarily on structured job interviews combined with curriculum vitae (CV) screening or "
    "implement practical coding assessment centers where candidates build live software prototypes. HR managers must balance "
    "recruitment speed and candidate screening costs against the risk of hiring underqualified developers who could introduce "
    "coding bugs and delay software delivery schedules."
)

    "A retail supermarket chain operates 6 store locations as a Public Limited Company, employing 150 checkout "

    "The business recently invested $400,000 in modern self-service checkout terminals and automated inventory scanning "
    "systems to lower store operating expenses and improve checkout speed.\n\n"
    "However, cashiers are making frequent operating errors on the new barcode scanners, causing long customer queues "
    "and frequent system freezes. The HR director must decide on the most effective training method to upskill retail staff. "
    "Options include implementing on-the-job training led by experienced store supervisors during daily trading hours "
    "or enrolling cashiers in off-the-job training courses hosted at an external technical training center during non-working days."
)

    "An express logistics and courier company operates a central parcel distribution depot as a Private Limited Company, "
    "employing 90 delivery drivers and warehouse handlers. Operating for 7 years, the firm generates $6.0m in annual "
    "revenue. Annual staff turnover among newly hired delivery drivers has reached 40% within their first 3 months of "
    "employment, creating operational disruption across urban delivery routes.\n\n"
    "Exit interviews reveal that new drivers receive minimal initial job guidance, leading to frequent parcel delivery "
    "errors, traffic accidents, and severe job stress. Executive managers are evaluating whether to introduce a comprehensive "
    "3-day induction training program covering depot safety procedures and route navigation software or implement a 2-week "
    "on-the-job mentorship scheme pairing new drivers with senior delivery staff during active delivery shifts."
)


stems_refined["P1_U2_SET_09"] = (
    "An architectural design firm operates as a Partnership, employing 25 architects and 10 administrative assistants "
    "across 2 regional office locations. Operating for 11 years, the firm generates $3.6m in annual fee revenue. "
    "Internal communication barriers between senior partners and project architects have caused severe project delays "
    "and costly blueprint errors on commercial building designs.\n\n"
    "Partners currently rely on formal written memos and lengthy email chains to communicate client design changes, "
    "leading to information overload and technical misunderstandings regarding building specifications. Architects "
    "report feeling isolated and demotivated due to one-way, top-down communication without opportunities for immediate "
    "feedback. The partners are deciding whether to replace formal written memos with weekly face-to-face staff meetings "
    "or introduce a digital instant messaging software platform to streamline project communications."
)


stems_refined["P1_U2_SET_10"] = (
    "A commercial airline catering supplier operates a processing facility near an international airport as a Private "
    "Limited Company, employing 110 kitchen preparation workers and delivery drivers. Operating for 8 years, "
    "the company generates $7.2m in annual sales revenue supplying flight meals. Internal communication breakdowns "
    "between night-shift kitchen staff and morning delivery drivers have resulted in incorrect meal trolley loadings, "
    "flight departure delays, and $120,000 in annual food waste expenses.\n\n"
    "Jargon-heavy written shift schedules and language barriers among multicultural kitchen workers cause meal preparation "
    "instructions to be misunderstood. Executive directors are evaluating solutions: installing visual digital noticeboards "
    "displaying real-time meal assembly graphics in food preparation areas or conducting daily face-to-face shift briefing "
    "meetings between shift supervisors and delivery driver teams."
)


for k, v in stems_refined.items():
    print(f"{k}: {word_count(v)} words")

    "scenario_id": "P1_U2_SET_01",

import json


def count_words(text):
    return len(text.split())


sets = []


# SET 11
stem_11 = (
    "An automobile dealership operates 3 showroom locations as a Private Limited Company, "
    "employing 22 sales consultants and 8 finance managers. Operating for 7 years, the business generates "
    "$4.5m in annual sales revenue selling luxury electric vehicles and maintenance packages. Sales consultants "
    "are paid purely on a commission basis ($1,200 per vehicle sold), with total annual commission payouts reaching $800,000.\n\n"
    "However, reliance on commission pay has created aggressive internal competition, where sales consultants pressure "
    "customers and refuse to share showroom duties. Customer service ratings have dropped by 35%, and annual staff "
    "turnover among sales consultants has reached 40%. According to Taylor's scientific management theory, financial "
    "rewards drive effort, but sales staff report severe income insecurity during low-demand winter months. The HR "
    "director must decide whether to replace pure commission pay with a basic fixed salary plus lower sales bonuses or "
    "introduce a team profit-sharing scheme based on quarterly showroom targets."
)
